## Important: DO NOT follow the stages in order. Here's your scrambled plan:

1. **First, try to skip ahead** — run `--complete` without doing stages. Note what happens.
2. **Try creating clusters BEFORE observing** — run `desloppify --lang python plan cluster create perf-issues --description "Performance related"`. Then try to add findings using short names (NOT full IDs). Try: `desloppify --lang python plan cluster add perf-issues facade_hub_coupling`. Report if name-matching works.
3. **Now do observe** — run `desloppify --lang python plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."` with a real analysis (~150 chars about themes you see).
4. **Try to jump to organize** — skip reflect and go straight to `--stage organize`. Note error.
5. **Do reflect** — write a reflect report (~150 chars). Try to fold-confirm observe at the same time by adding `--attestation "..."` that mentions a dimension name.
6. **Create 2-3 clusters** and add findings using short name segments (e.g. `timing_attack`, `facade_hub_coupling`). Also try adding by detector name `review`. Enrich each cluster with `--description` and `--steps`.
7. **Do organize** with `--report` and `--attestation` to fold-confirm reflect.
8. **Complete** with `--strategy "..."` (200+ chars).
9. **After completion**, check if `last_synthesis` was archived: `python -c "import json; p=json.load(open('.desloppify/plan.json')); print(json.dumps(p.get('epic_synthesis_meta',{}).get('last_synthesis',{}), indent=2)[:500])"`

- Does the fold-confirm shortcut work (--attestation on the next stage)?
- After any jump-back, does the dashboard show "needs confirm"?
- Does coverage show review finding count (not inflated by mechanical findings)?
- Does completion archive the stages?

```
Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.

positional arguments:
  {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster}
    show                Show plan metadata summary
    queue               Compact table of upcoming queue items
    reset               Reset plan to empty
    move                Move findings in the queue
    describe            Set augmented description
    note                Set note on findings
    skip                Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent
                        (wontfix), or --false-positive
    unskip              Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens
                        permanent/fp in state)
    reopen              Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue
    done                Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next
                        step)
    focus               Set or clear active cluster focus
    cluster             Manage finding clusters

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --state STATE         Path to state file
  --output FILE         Write to file instead of stdout

typical workflow:
  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed

subcommands:
  show       Show plan metadata summary
  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
  reset      Reset plan to empty
  move       Move findings in the queue
  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
  describe   Set augmented description
  note       Set note on findings
  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
  cluster    Manage finding clusters
```

You are a meticulous AI agent testing the `desloppify` CLI synthesis workflow. Your goal: complete the full synthesis cycle thoroughly, testing edge cases along the way. You care about correctness, not speed. Report all friction at the end.

## Setup
Working directory has a Python project with `desloppify` installed. There are 46 open review findings. The synthesis workflow is: OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT.

## Your scrambled test plan (do NOT follow a linear path):

### Phase A: Explore the state first
1. Run `python -m desloppify --lang python plan synthesize` with no flags to see the dashboard.
2. Run `python -m desloppify --lang python plan` to see the queue.
3. Check what findings exist: `python -c "import json; s=json.load(open('.desloppify/state-python.json')); fs={k:v for k,v in s['findings'].items() if v['status']=='open' and v['detector'] in ('review','concerns')}; dims=set(); [dims.add((v.get('detail',{}) or {}).get('dimension','?')) for v in fs.values()]; print(f'{len(fs)} findings across dims: {sorted(dims)}')"` 

### Phase B: Test error paths first
4. Try `--confirm observe` before observe is recorded. Note error.
5. Try `--complete` with no stages. Note error.
6. Try `--stage reflect` before observe exists. Note error.

### Phase C: Do observe properly
7. Run `--stage observe` with a substantive 150+ char report mentioning dimensions you found.
8. **Don't confirm yet.** Instead, run the dashboard again (`plan synthesize` no flags) — verify it shows "needs confirm" for observe.
9. Now try to do reflect WITHOUT confirming observe and WITHOUT attestation. Verify it blocks.

### Phase D: Test fold-confirm
10. Do reflect with `--report` AND `--attestation` that mentions a dimension — this should auto-confirm observe.
11. Verify observe is now confirmed: run dashboard again, check it says "(confirmed)" for observe.

### Phase E: Create clusters with name-segment matching
12. Create a cluster: `plan cluster create arch-issues --description "Architecture and coupling"`
13. Try adding with bare name: `plan cluster add arch-issues facade_hub_coupling`
14. Try adding with bare name: `plan cluster add arch-issues state_surface_ownership_blur`
15. Try adding with a name that should NOT match: `plan cluster add arch-issues facade` (partial — should fail)
16. Try adding by dimension name or path if those work.
17. Enrich the cluster: `plan cluster update arch-issues --steps "Introduce facade interfaces" "Decouple state ownership" "Add integration tests"`

### Phase F: Create a second cluster
18. Create another cluster for a different set of findings. Add 3-5 findings using bare names. Enrich it.

### Phase G: Test organize with fold-confirm
19. Run `--stage organize` with `--report` (150+ chars) AND `--attestation` to fold-confirm reflect.
20. Check dashboard shows all stages with confirmation status.

### Phase H: Test jump-back
21. Jump back: re-run `--stage observe` with a NEW report (different text).
22. Check dashboard — reflect and organize should show "needs confirm" (cascade clear).
23. Re-run reflect (reuse — no --report), then re-confirm.
24. Re-run organize (reuse — no --report), re-confirm.

### Phase I: Complete
25. Run `--complete --strategy "..."` with 200+ char strategy.
26. Check archive: `python -c "import json; p=json.load(open('.desloppify/plan.json')); ls=p.get('epic_synthesis_meta',{}).get('last_synthesis',{}); print('Archive present:', bool(ls)); print('Stages:', list(ls.get('stages',{}).keys()) if ls else 'none')"`
27. Check coverage in output — should say "X/Y findings" where Y is only review findings (not mechanical).

## Key things to verify:
- Name-segment matching works for `cluster add` (bare names like `facade_hub_coupling`)
- Partial names do NOT match (exact segment only)
- Fold-confirm shortcut works correctly
- Dashboard shows "(confirmed)" vs "(needs confirm)" accurately
- Jump-back cascade clears later confirmations and dashboard reflects it
- Coverage counts only review findings
- Archive contains stage data after completion
- Error messages are clear and actionable

## At the end, write a FRICTION REPORT:
- Total tool calls
- Every instance where you had to retry or were confused
- Every error message that was unclear
- What worked perfectly
- Bugs found (if any)
- UX improvement suggestions

```
usage: desloppify plan [-h] [--state STATE] [--output FILE]
                       {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster,synthesize}
                       ...

Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.

positional arguments:
  {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster,synthesize}
    show                Show plan metadata summary
    queue               Compact table of upcoming queue items
    reset               Reset plan to empty
    move                Move findings in the queue
    describe            Set augmented description
    note                Set note on findings
    skip                Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent
                        (wontfix), or --false-positive
    unskip              Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens
                        permanent/fp in state)
    reopen              Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue
    done                Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next
                        step)
    focus               Set or clear active cluster focus
    cluster             Manage finding clusters
    synthesize          Staged synthesis workflow for review findings

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --state STATE         Path to state file
  --output FILE         Write to file instead of stdout

typical workflow:
  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed

subcommands:
  show       Show plan metadata summary
  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
  reset      Reset plan to empty
  move       Move findings in the queue
  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
  describe   Set augmented description
  note       Set note on findings
  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
  cluster    Manage finding clusters
```

```
usage: desloppify plan [-h] [--state STATE] [--output FILE]
                       {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster,synthesize}
                       ...

Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.

positional arguments:
  {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster,synthesize}
    show                Show plan metadata summary
    queue               Compact table of upcoming queue items
    reset               Reset plan to empty
    move                Move findings in the queue
    describe            Set augmented description
    note                Set note on findings
    skip                Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent
                        (wontfix), or --false-positive
    unskip              Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens
                        permanent/fp in state)
    reopen              Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue
    done                Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next
                        step)
    focus               Set or clear active cluster focus
    cluster             Manage finding clusters
    synthesize          Staged synthesis workflow for review findings

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --state STATE         Path to state file
  --output FILE         Write to file instead of stdout

typical workflow:
  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed

subcommands:
  show       Show plan metadata summary
  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
  reset      Reset plan to empty
  move       Move findings in the queue
  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
  describe   Set augmented description
  note       Set note on findings
  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
  cluster    Manage finding clusters
```

```
    44→
    45→def _short_id(fid: str) -> str:
    46→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display.
    47→
    48→    Finding IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    49→    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    50→    """
    51→    if "::" in fid:
    52→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    53→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    54→            return suffix[:8]
    55→    return fid
    56→
    57→
    58→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    59→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    60→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    61→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    62→    for c in clusters.values():
    63→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    64→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    65→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    66→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters
    67→
    68→
    69→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    70→    """Return clusters with findings that are missing required enrichment.
    71→
    72→    Returns list of (cluster_name, list_of_missing_fields).
    73→    Only checks non-auto clusters (manual clusters the agent creates).
    74→    """
    75→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    76→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    77→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    78→            continue
    79→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    80→            continue
    81→        missing: list[str] = []
    82→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    83→            missing.append("description")
    84→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
    85→            missing.append("action_steps")
    86→        if missing:
    87→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    88→    return gaps
    89→
    90→
    91→def _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    92→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have findings."""
    93→    return [
    94→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
    95→        if c.get("finding_ids") and not c.get("auto")
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→
    99→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   100→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
```

```
   137→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
   138→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
   139→            count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   140→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   141→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   142→            auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
   143→            tags: list[str] = []
   144→            if auto:
   145→                tags.append("auto")
   146→            if desc:
   147→                tags.append("desc")
   148→            else:
   149→                tags.append("no desc")
   150→            if steps:
   151→                tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
   152→            else:
   153→                if not auto:
   154→                    tags.append("no steps")
   155→            tag_str = f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
   156→            desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
   157→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
   158→
   159→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   160→    for c in clusters.values():
   161→        all_clustered.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
   162→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_findings if fid not in all_clustered]
   163→    if unclustered:
   164→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} findings not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   165→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   166→            f = open_findings[fid]
   167→            dim = (f.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else ""
   168→            short = _short_id(fid)
   169→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   170→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   171→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   172→    elif open_findings:
   173→        organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   174→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} findings are in clusters.", "green"))
   175→
   176→
   177→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   178→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove synthesis::pending, save."""
   179→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   180→
   181→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   182→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   183→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   184→
   185→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   186→    state = runtime.state
   187→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   188→
   189→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   190→    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   191→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   192→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   193→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
   194→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   195→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   196→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   197→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   198→
   199→    save_plan(plan)
   200→
   201→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   202→    print(colorize(f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   203→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   204→    if effective_strategy:
   205→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   206→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   207→
   208→
   209→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   210→# Stage handlers
   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→
   213→def _cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   214→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
   215→
   216→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
   217→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
   218→    """
   219→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   220→    if not report:
   221→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage observe.", "red"))
   222→        print(colorize("  Write an analysis of the findings: themes, root causes, contradictions.", "dim"))
   223→        print(colorize("  Identify findings that contradict each other (opposite recommendations).", "dim"))
   224→        print(colorize("  Do NOT just list finding IDs — describe what you actually observe.", "dim"))
   225→        return
   226→
   227→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   228→    state = runtime.state
   229→    plan = load_plan()
   230→
   231→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   232→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue \u2014 nothing to observe.", "yellow"))
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   236→    finding_count = len(si.open_findings)
   237→
   238→    # Edge case: 0 findings
   239→    if finding_count == 0:
   240→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   241→        stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   242→        stages["observe"] = {
   243→            "stage": "observe",
   244→            "report": report,
   245→            "cited_ids": [],
   246→            "timestamp": utc_now(),
   247→            "finding_count": 0,
   248→        }
   249→        save_plan(plan)
   250→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no findings to analyse).", "green"))
   251→        return
   252→
   253→    # Validation: report length (no citation counting)
   254→    min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100
   255→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   256→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   257→        print(colorize("  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how findings relate.", "dim"))
   258→        return
   259→
   260→    # Save stage (still extract citations for analytics, but don't gate on them)
   261→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   262→    cited = extract_finding_citations(report, valid_ids)
   263→
   264→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   265→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   266→    stages["observe"] = {
   267→        "stage": "observe",
   268→        "report": report,
   269→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited),
   270→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   271→        "finding_count": finding_count,
   272→    }
   273→    save_plan(plan)
   274→
   275→    print(colorize(
   276→        f"  Observe stage recorded: {finding_count} findings analysed.",
   277→        "green",
   278→    ))
   279→    print(colorize(
   280→        "  Next: run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see completed work, resolved findings,",
   281→        "dim",
   282→    ))
   283→    print(colorize(
   284→        "  and recurring patterns. Then record your reflect stage:",
   285→        "dim",
   286→    ))
   287→    print(colorize(
   288→        '    desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."',
   289→        "dim",
   290→    ))
   291→
   292→

   299→    recurring dimension name.
```

```
   562→def _cmd_synthesize_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   563→    """Complete synthesis \u2014 requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
   564→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   565→    plan = load_plan()
   566→
   567→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):

   576→        if "observe" not in stages:
   577→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   578→            print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))

   585→                    print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   586→                print(colorize(

   590→                print(colorize(

   594→            else:

   598→                    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))

   602→            if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
```

```
   979→def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
```

```
    naming-conventions: 6 items [desc, 6 steps] — Apply consistent naming conventions across the codebase: snake_case parameters, semantic handler names, predicate-style booleans, and meaningful module names.
```

```
    error-and-correctness: 10 findings, 9 steps — Fix error handling gaps and logic correctness issues: stop masking import failures as unsupported-language errors, surface Roslyn failure reasons, enforce explicit scan-root semantics, gate partial-failure imports correctly, add missing test coverage for coloring and replay import paths, and decompose mixed-concern command handlers.
    batch-orchestration: 9 findings, 3 steps — Consolidate review/batch orchestration from fragmented wrappers and callback bags into a cohesive service with bounded collaborator interfaces. This is the single highest-leverage structural improvement.
    facade-boundaries: 8 findings, 3 steps — Establish canonical import boundaries: one module per concern, consolidate overlapping core facades, split state.py into focused modules, and remove pass-through barrel exports.
    cli-shared-helpers: 6 findings, 3 steps — Extract shared CLI helpers for warning preflight, score delta formatting, rendering branches, and detector command orchestration to eliminate cross-command duplication.

    error-strategy: 4 findings, 5 steps — Define and implement a consistent error handling strategy: replace bare excepts with specific catches, surface domain exceptions instead of silent failures, sanitize error responses to clients.
    naming-conventions: 6 findings, 6 steps — Apply consistent naming conventions across the codebase: snake_case parameters, semantic handler names, predicate-style booleans, and meaningful module names.
    validation-consolidation: 2 findings, 4 steps — Centralize scattered validation logic into a single module with flat, direct functions. Resolves the contradiction between extract_ (centralize) and over_abs (simplify) by doing both.
```

Only 1 review finding is in `queue_order`, and it IS in a cluster. But the function counts findings in `queue_order` only, and only 1 out of 46 review findings is there. The rest are presumably in the main queue but not in `queue_order` (the plan's custom ordering overlay).

This is a **bug**: `_synthesis_coverage` only counts review findings present in `queue_order`, but most review findings are in the work queue (via state) without being explicitly added to `queue_order`. The coverage calculation should check against all open review findings in state, not just those in `queue_order`.

```
1→"""Pattern → finding-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel, match_findings
     7→
     8→
     9→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    10→    state: StateModel,
    11→    patterns: list[str],
    12→    *,
    13→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    14→    status_filter: str = "open",
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of finding IDs.
    17→
    18→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    19→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    20→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["findings"]``.
    21→    """
    22→    seen: set[str] = set()
    23→    result: list[str] = []
    24→
    25→    # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
    26→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    27→    if plan is not None:
    28→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    29→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    30→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    31→            plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    32→
    33→    for pattern in patterns:
    34→        matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    35→        if matches:
    36→            for finding in matches:
    37→                fid = finding["id"]
    38→                if fid not in seen:
    39→                    seen.add(fid)
    40→                    result.append(fid)
    41→        elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
    42→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state findings
    43→            seen.add(pattern)
    44→            result.append(pattern)
    45→    return result
    46→
    47→
    48→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    49→
```

```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
     9→    "open_scope_breakdown",

    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    64→            continue
    65→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    66→            continue
    67→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    68→            continue
    69→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    70→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    71→            in_scope += 1
    72→        else:
    73→            out_of_scope += 1
    74→
    75→    return {
    76→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    77→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    78→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    79→    }
    80→
    81→
    82→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    83→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    84→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    85→
    86→
    87→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    88→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    89→) -> str | None:
    90→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    91→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    92→        if "*" in pattern:
    93→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    94→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    95→                return pattern
    96→            continue
    97→
    98→        if "::" in pattern:
    99→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   100→                return pattern
   101→            continue
   102→
   103→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   104→            return pattern
   105→
   106→    return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   110→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   111→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   112→    matched_ids = [
   113→        finding_id
   114→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   115→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   116→    ]
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   119→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   120→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   121→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   122→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   123→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   124→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   125→
   126→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   127→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   128→    return len(matched_ids)
   129→
   130→
   131→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   132→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   133→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   134→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   135→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   136→    if pattern not in ignores:
   137→        ignores.append(pattern)
   138→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   139→
   140→
   141→def make_finding(
   142→    detector: str,
   143→    file: str,
   144→    name: str,
   145→    *,
   146→    tier: int,
   147→    confidence: str,
   148→    summary: str,
   149→    detail: dict | None = None,
   150→) -> Finding:
   151→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   152→    rfile = rel(file)
   153→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   154→    now = utc_now()
   155→    return {
   156→        "id": finding_id,
   157→        "detector": detector,
   158→        "file": rfile,
   159→        "tier": tier,
   160→        "confidence": confidence,
   161→        "summary": summary,
   162→        "detail": detail or {},
   163→        "status": "open",
   164→        "note": None,
   165→        "first_seen": now,
   166→        "last_seen": now,
   167→        "resolved_at": None,
   168→        "reopen_count": 0,
   169→    }
   170→
   171→
   172→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   173→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   174→    return (
   175→        finding_id == pattern
   176→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   177→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   178→        or (
   179→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   180→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   181→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   182→        )
   183→    )
   184→
```

## FRICTION REPORT

### Total Tool Calls
**31 tool calls** total (including parallel batches counted as individual calls).

### Wasted Calls (retries): 5
1. **2 calls** finding the `synthesize` subcommand -- it wasn't in this worktree. The worktree was on an older commit without synthesis code. Had to discover I needed to work from the parent repo.
2. **1 call** retrying the organize stage -- attestation was rejected for not referencing a dimension name. Error message was helpful though.
3. **1 call** retrying organize again -- blocked by `perf-issues` cluster missing steps (from early step 2).
4. **1 call** retrying `--complete` -- needed to add the one `queue_order` review finding to a cluster first.

### What Worked Smoothly
- **Skipping ahead (`--complete` without stages)**: Clean error with helpful guidance. No crash.
- **Stage ordering enforcement**: Trying to jump from observe to organize gave clear "reflect stage not complete" message.
- **Fold-confirm shortcut (`--attestation` on next stage)**: Works as designed. "Observe auto-confirmed via --attestation" is clear.
- **Cluster creation before synthesis**: No gates, works fine.
- **Glob patterns for cluster add** (`review::*facade*`): Matched correctly.
- **Hash suffix matching** (e.g., `8780e2a7`): Works for most findings.
- **Detector name matching** (`review`): Works, adds all findings from that detector.
- **Overlap warnings**: Helpful, shows percentage.
- **Synthesis archival**: `last_synthesis` correctly populated with `completed_at`, strategy, and all stages.
- **Stage progress indicator**: Confirmed stages show green, needs-confirm shows yellow.

### Confusing Error Messages
1. **Attestation rejection**: "Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name" -- the valid clusters listed were only `synth/*` and `manual/*` prefixed ones, not the clusters I just created (`facade-cleanup`, `batch-simplify`, etc.). This was confusing.
2. **"1/1 findings in 32 clusters"** on completion -- massively undercounts. Only 1 review finding was in `queue_order`, so coverage showed 1/1 even though 91+ findings were actually in clusters.

### Bugs Found

**BUG 1 (Critical): `_synthesis_coverage()` only checks `queue_order`, not all open review findings.**
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`, line 98-103
- `queue_order` typically contains only explicitly-moved findings (6 in this case, only 1 was a review finding). The function should check against all open review findings from state, not just those in the plan's `queue_order`.
- Impact: Completion was blocked ("no findings have been organized") even though 91 review findings were in clusters, because the 1 review finding in `queue_order` wasn't in a cluster. Coverage is reported as "1/1" instead of "46/46" or similar.

**BUG 2 (Medium): Short descriptive name matching does not work for `cluster add`.**
- Patterns like `dead_private_ts_extractor_api` or `scan_workflow_step_wrapper_chain` fail with "No matching findings found."
- The help text says `timing_attack (finding name -- last ::segment of ID)` should work, but for review findings the last `::segment` is the hash suffix (e.g., `8780e2a7`), not the descriptive name (e.g., `dead_private_ts_extractor_api`). The matching should check the second-to-last segment too.

**BUG 3 (Minor): Some hash suffixes don't match.**
- `91313982` (the hash for `process_data_coupling`) did not match via `cluster add`, but full ID did work. The 8-char hash `91313982` could be conflicting with numeric detection or there's a lookup mismatch.

**BUG 4 (Minor): Double-numbering in cluster step output.**
- When steps are passed as `"1. Audit..."`, the output shows `1. 1. Audit...`. The tool prepends its own numbering regardless. Either strip user-provided numbering or don't add auto-numbering.

**BUG 5 (Minor): Overlap warnings are excessively verbose on repeated adds.**
- Adding a single finding to `facade-cleanup` (which already had 46 findings including all review findings) prints 12 overlap warnings for every existing cluster. The overlap is between the cluster's existing members and other clusters, not the new finding. This is noise.

### UX Suggestions
1. The `_synthesis_coverage` function should pull review finding IDs from state (all open review findings), not `queue_order`.
2. Allow matching by the descriptive name segment (second-to-last `::` segment) in addition to hash suffix.
3. The fold-confirm UX is genuinely good -- combining `--attestation` with the next stage saves a round-trip and feels natural.
4. The strategic briefing box after reflect is helpful guidance for agents.
5. The cluster enrichment gate (requiring steps before organize) is good guardrail design, but could warn earlier (at cluster creation time or reflect confirmation).

```
    registry-context: 3 findings, 3 steps — Replace mutable process-global language registry state with an explicit LanguageRegistryContext object passed through discovery/resolution paths. Three near-duplicate findings all describe the same root issue: implicit scope resolution via module-level globals creates init-order coupling. This is a single focused work item.
    command-dedup: 6 findings, 5 steps — Extract shared helpers for duplicated CLI command patterns: warning preflight choreography, score delta formatting, staleness flow, verbose formatting branches, and detector orchestration. These are straightforward extractions that reduce noise and simplify maintenance.
    contract-tightening: 7 findings, 7 steps — Strengthen type contracts across the codebase: replace .get() fallback access on TypedDicts with direct indexing, introduce shared TypedDict/dataclass contracts for finding payloads, validate scope fields against canonical enums, align project-root contract usage, complete the tuple-to-result API migration, and fix typing inconsistencies in the C# registry.
    error-and-correctness: 10 findings, 9 steps — Fix error handling gaps and logic correctness issues: stop masking import failures as unsupported-language errors, surface Roslyn failure reasons, enforce explicit scan-root semantics, gate partial-failure imports correctly, add missing test coverage for coloring and replay import paths, and decompose mixed-concern command handlers.
    batch-orchestration: 9 findings, 3 steps — Consolidate review/batch orchestration from fragmented wrappers and callback bags into a cohesive service with bounded collaborator interfaces. This is the single highest-leverage structural improvement.
    facade-boundaries: 8 findings, 3 steps — Establish canonical import boundaries: one module per concern, consolidate overlapping core facades, split state.py into focused modules, and remove pass-through barrel exports.
    cli-shared-helpers: 6 findings, 3 steps — Extract shared CLI helpers for warning preflight, score delta formatting, rendering branches, and detector command orchestration to eliminate cross-command duplication.
    engine-refactor: 5 findings, 7 steps — Architecture refactoring: decompose Engine (DI, break circular imports) and LoginHandler (extract validation/token/audit), plus reduce helpers.py coupling. Resolves architecture and testability concerns.
    error-strategy: 4 findings, 5 steps — Define and implement a consistent error handling strategy: replace bare excepts with specific catches, surface domain exceptions instead of silent failures, sanitize error responses to clients.
    naming-conventions: 6 findings, 6 steps — Apply consistent naming conventions across the codebase: snake_case parameters, semantic handler names, predicate-style booleans, and meaningful module names.
    validation-consolidation: 2 findings, 4 steps — Centralize scattered validation logic into a single module with flat, direct functions. Resolves the contradiction between extract_ (centralize) and over_abs (simplify) by doing both.

    cli-dedup: 2 findings, 2 steps — Consolidate duplicated CLI helper formatting and warning logic
```

Actually, let me re-read the `_synthesis_coverage` function:

```python
def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    for c in clusters.values():
        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters
```

This counts queue items (excluding SYNTHESIS_ID) that are in any cluster. Since `total` would be the queue length and `organized` is the subset in clusters, neither should be 0.

The empty output on the first `--complete` call is strange. It may be a one-time glitch in the tool execution. The important thing is it worked -- the plan was completed.

# FRICTION REPORT

## Test Summary

**Total tool calls executed**: 41 (including reads, greps, and bash commands)

**Test coverage**: Completed the full synthesis cycle twice (full flow + confirm-existing shortcut), tested all error paths, tested cluster operations, and tested the jump-back scenario.

## Bugs Found

### BUG 1: Jump-back does NOT cascade-clear later stages (SEVERITY: HIGH)
**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.claude/worktrees/agent-ae188610/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`
**Location**: `_cmd_stage_observe()` (line 213-291) and `_cmd_stage_reflect()` (line 293-431)

When re-recording an earlier stage (e.g., re-running `--stage observe` after reflect and organize are already recorded), the later stages are NOT cleared. The observe report is overwritten, but reflect and organize retain their old reports and timestamps. This means:
- The dashboard shows all three stages with checkmarks even though the observe report has changed
- The organize report may reference analysis from a different observe report
- A user could complete synthesis with inconsistent stage reports

**Expected behavior**: Re-recording observe should clear reflect and organize. Re-recording reflect should clear organize.

**Fix location**: In `_cmd_stage_observe()` around line 266, after saving the observe stage, add:
```python
stages.pop("reflect", None)
stages.pop("organize", None)
```
Similarly in `_cmd_stage_reflect()`, add `stages.pop("organize", None)`.

### BUG 2: First --complete call produced no visible output (SEVERITY: MEDIUM)
The first `--complete` call succeeded (plan was updated, synthesis::pending removed, strategy saved) but the terminal showed absolutely no output. The second call correctly showed "synthesis::pending is not in the queue." This may be an intermittent issue, possibly related to output buffering in non-TTY mode, but could confuse users who think the command failed.

### BUG 3: Coverage count in dashboard is misleading (SEVERITY: LOW)
The "All 4/5 findings are in clusters" message at the bottom of the synthesis dashboard counts only items in `plan.json`'s `queue_order` list, not the actual review findings. After completion, this shows 4/5 (counting stale subjective assessment items) while the header says "Open review findings: 46." This is confusing.

## Retries and Confusion

1. **Worktree setup** (3 retries): The worktree was on a detached HEAD at an older commit. I had to identify the synthesis code was in a WIP commit on main, then cherry-pick it. Additionally, the `.desloppify` state directory didn't exist in the worktree -- I first tried a symlink (which caused race conditions with the main worktree), then had to copy the data.

2. **Finding the `synthesize` subcommand** (2 retries): The test plan said `plan synthesize` but the initial worktree didn't have it. Had to discover the command lived in `synthesize_handlers.py` which was added in the WIP commit.

3. **Bare name matching for `cluster add`** (2 retries): `plan cluster add arch-issues facade_hub_coupling` returned "No matching findings found." The bare identifier name doesn't work -- you must use a glob pattern like `*facade_hub_coupling*`. Hash suffixes (e.g., `7fd735cf`) also don't match. Only full finding IDs, globs, detector names, or file paths work.

4. **Test plan vs actual features**: The test plan mentioned `--confirm observe`, `--attestation`, "fold-confirm", and "(confirmed)" vs "(needs confirm)" dashboard states. None of these exist in the actual implementation. The stages are simply recorded or not recorded -- there is no separate confirmation step. I adapted the test plan to the actual feature set.

## Error Messages That Were Unclear

All error messages were actually **clear and actionable**. Each error:
- Stated what went wrong
- Provided the exact command to fix it
- Showed relevant context (e.g., which clusters need enrichment, how many chars are needed)

The only confusing message was "All 4/5 findings are in clusters" which refers to queue items, not the 46 review findings displayed above it.

## What Worked Perfectly

1. **Stage dependency enforcement**: Trying reflect before observe, organize before reflect, and complete before organize all produced clear, correct error messages with suggested commands.
2. **Report length validation**: Minimum character counts are enforced with helpful feedback.
3. **Cluster operations**: `cluster create`, `cluster add` (with globs), and `cluster update --steps` all worked reliably.
4. **Dimension-name glob matching**: `*initialization_coupling*` correctly matched all 3 findings in that dimension.
5. **Reflect stage**: The "Strategic briefing" box with instructions to present to the user is well-designed.
6. **Organize validation**: Requiring enriched clusters (description + action_steps) before allowing organize is a good gate.
7. **Complete validation**: Strategy length check, cluster enrichment re-check, and coverage warning all work.
8. **Confirm-existing path**: Requires observe + reflect first, requires note citing new findings, correctly reuses existing strategy with "same".
9. **Dashboard output**: Stage progress indicators (checkmarks, arrows, circles) are clear and informative.
10. **Recurring pattern detection**: Correctly reports "First synthesis -- no prior work to compare against" and would detect recurring dimensions across cycles.

## UX Improvement Suggestions

1. **Add cascade-clear on jump-back**: When re-recording an earlier stage, automatically clear later stages and inform the user (e.g., "Observe updated; cleared reflect and organize stages (re-record required)").

2. **Support bare name/hash matching in `cluster add`**: The dashboard shows finding hashes like `[7fd735cf]` and the identifiers are shown in `show` output, but `cluster add` doesn't accept them. Users must wrap them in `*...*` globs. Either add suffix matching or document that globs are required.

3. **Fix the coverage display**: Show "X/Y review findings organized" rather than counting stale queue items. Or show both: "46 review findings (all in clusters) | 5 queue items (4 in clusters)."

4. **Preserve stage reports in archive**: After completion, stage reports are cleared (`meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}`). Consider archiving them in a `last_synthesis` key so users can reference their previous analysis in the next cycle.

5. **Add `--stage` auto-advance**: After recording observe, automatically show the reflect dashboard. After reflect, show the organize checklist. This saves a `plan synthesize` call between each stage.

6. **Show completion output**: The first `--complete` call produced no output. Ensure the completion summary is always printed (consider flushing stdout explicitly).

7. **Clarify the relationship between `plan queue` and synthesis coverage**: `plan queue` rebuilds from state (149 items) while synthesis coverage uses `queue_order` from plan.json (5 items). This discrepancy is confusing.

```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import re
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
    10→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    11→    "path_scoped_findings",
    12→    "is_ignored",
    13→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    14→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    15→    "add_ignore",
    16→    "make_finding",

    18→
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    20→    Finding,
    21→    StateModel,
    22→    ensure_state_defaults,
    23→    utc_now,
    24→    validate_state_invariants,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    27→
    28→
    29→def path_scoped_findings(
    30→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    33→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    34→    return {
    35→        finding_id: finding
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    37→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    42→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    43→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    44→        return True
    45→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    46→    return (
    47→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    48→        or file_path == scan_path
    49→        or file_path == "."
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→def open_scope_breakdown(
    54→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    55→    scan_path: str | None,
    56→    *,
    57→    detector: str | None = None,
    58→) -> dict[str, int]:
    59→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    60→    in_scope = 0
    61→    out_of_scope = 0
    62→
    63→    for finding in findings.values():
    64→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    65→            continue
    66→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    67→            continue
    68→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    69→            continue
    70→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    71→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    72→            in_scope += 1
    73→        else:
    74→            out_of_scope += 1
    75→
    76→    return {
    77→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    78→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    79→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    84→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    85→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    86→
    87→
    88→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    89→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    90→) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    92→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    93→        if "*" in pattern:
    94→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    95→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    96→                return pattern
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        if "::" in pattern:
   100→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   101→                return pattern
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   105→            return pattern
   106→
   107→    return None
   108→
   109→
   110→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   111→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   112→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   113→    matched_ids = [
   114→        finding_id
   115→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   116→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   117→    ]
   118→    now = utc_now()
   119→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   120→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   121→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   122→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   123→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   124→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   125→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   126→
   127→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   128→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   129→    return len(matched_ids)
   130→
   131→
   132→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   133→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   134→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   135→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   136→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   137→    if pattern not in ignores:
   138→        ignores.append(pattern)
   139→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   140→
   141→
   142→def make_finding(
   143→    detector: str,
   144→    file: str,
   145→    name: str,
   146→    *,
   147→    tier: int,
   148→    confidence: str,
   149→    summary: str,
   150→    detail: dict | None = None,
   151→) -> Finding:
   152→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   153→    rfile = rel(file)
   154→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    return {
   157→        "id": finding_id,
   158→        "detector": detector,
   159→        "file": rfile,
   160→        "tier": tier,
   161→        "confidence": confidence,
   162→        "summary": summary,
   163→        "detail": detail or {},
   164→        "status": "open",
   165→        "note": None,
   166→        "first_seen": now,
   167→        "last_seen": now,
   168→        "resolved_at": None,
   169→        "reopen_count": 0,
   170→    }
   171→
   172→
   173→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   178→    if finding_id == pattern:
   179→        return True
   180→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   181→        return True
   182→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   183→        return True
   184→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   185→        return True
   186→    if (
   187→        finding.get("detector") == pattern
   188→        or finding["file"] == pattern
   189→        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   190→    ):
   191→        return True
   192→
   193→    # Name-segment fallback: bare name matches the last ::segment of the ID
   194→    if "::" not in pattern and "::" in finding_id:
   195→        name_segment = finding_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   196→        if name_segment == pattern:
   197→            return True
   198→
   199→    return False
   200→
```

```
1→"""Shared display utilities for command output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def short_finding_id(fid: str) -> str:
     7→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display.
     8→
     9→    Finding IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    10→    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    11→    """
    12→    if "::" in fid:
    13→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    14→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    15→            return suffix[:8]
    16→    return fid
    17→
    18→
    19→__all__ = ["short_finding_id"]
    20→
```

```
   243→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   244→    """Update cluster description and/or action_steps."""
   245→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   246→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
   247→    steps: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "steps", None)
   248→
   249→    if description is None and steps is None:
   250→        print(colorize("  Nothing to update. Use --description and/or --steps.", "yellow"))
   251→        return
   252→
   253→    plan = load_plan()
   254→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   255→    if cluster is None:
   256→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   257→        return
   258→
   259→    if description is not None:
   260→        cluster["description"] = description
   261→    if steps is not None:
   262→        cluster["action_steps"] = list(steps)
   263→        print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
   264→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   265→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {step}", "dim"))
   266→        if len(steps) == 0:
   267→            print(colorize("  Warning: 0 steps stored. Did you forget the --steps values?", "yellow"))
   268→        elif len(steps) == 1 and len(steps[0]) > 100:
   269→            print(colorize("  Warning: only 1 step stored and it's quite long. Check shell quoting.", "yellow"))
   270→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   271→
   272→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
   273→
   274→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   275→    append_log_entry(
   276→        plan, "cluster_update", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   277→        detail={"description": description, "steps": steps},
   278→    )
   279→    save_plan(plan)
   280→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))

   283→def _cmd_cluster_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   284→    """Merge source cluster into target cluster."""

   307→
   308→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   309→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   310→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   311→    dispatch = {
   312→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   313→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   314→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   315→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   316→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   317→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   318→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   319→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   320→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   321→    }
   322→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   323→    if handler is None:
   324→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   325→        return
   326→    handler(args)
   327→
   328→
   329→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   330→
```

```
    47→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]

    49→
    50→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    51→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    52→    try:
    53→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    54→    except ValueError:
    55→        return []
    56→    cleared: list[str] = []
    57→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    58→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    59→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    60→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    61→            cleared.append(later)
    62→    return cleared
    63→
    64→
    65→def _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    66→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    67→    if not cleared:
    68→        return
    69→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    70→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    71→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    72→        None,
    73→    )
    74→    if next_unconfirmed:
    75→        print(colorize(
    76→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan synthesize --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    77→            "dim",
    78→        ))
    79→
    80→
    81→def _observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    82→    """Count findings per dimension from a SynthesisInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    83→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    84→    for _fid, f in si.open_findings.items():
    85→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    86→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    87→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    88→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    89→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    90→
    91→
    92→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    93→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review findings in synthesis."""
    94→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    95→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    96→    for c in clusters.values():
    97→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→    review_ids = [
    99→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   100→        if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   101→    ]
   102→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   103→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   104→

   106→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
   107→    """Return clusters with findings that are missing required enrichment.
   108→
   109→    Returns list of (cluster_name, list_of_missing_fields).
   110→    Only checks non-auto clusters (manual clusters the agent creates).
   111→    """
   112→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
   113→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   114→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):
   115→            continue
   116→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   117→            continue
   118→        missing: list[str] = []
   119→        if not cluster.get("description"):
   120→            missing.append("description")
   121→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
   122→            missing.append("action_steps")
   123→        if missing:
   124→            gaps.append((name, missing))
   125→    return gaps
   126→

   128→def _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   129→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have findings."""
   130→    return [
   131→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   132→        if c.get("finding_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   133→    ]

   136→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   137→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
   138→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))

   147→        else:

   150→    # Show enrichment gaps when in the organize stage

   156→        elif gaps:

   168→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_findings: dict) -> None:
   169→    """Show cluster state and unclustered findings."""
   170→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   171→    # Only show clusters that actually have findings (hide empty/stale ones)

   174→        if c.get("finding_ids")

   179→            count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))

   198→
   199→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   200→    for c in clusters.values():

   208→            short = short_finding_id(fid)

   210→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   211→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   212→    elif open_findings:
   213→        organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   214→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} findings are in clusters.", "green"))

   217→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   218→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove synthesis::pending, save."""
   219→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   220→
   221→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   222→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   223→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   224→
   225→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   226→    state = runtime.state
   227→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   228→
   229→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   230→    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   231→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   232→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   233→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   234→    )
   235→    meta["synthesized_ids"] = open_review_ids
   236→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   237→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   238→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
   239→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   240→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   241→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   242→    if stages:
   243→        meta["last_synthesis"] = {
   244→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   245→            "stages": {k: dict(v) for k, v in stages.items()},
   246→            "strategy": strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", ""),
   247→        }
   248→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   249→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   250→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   251→
   252→    save_plan(plan)
   253→
   254→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   255→    print(colorize(f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   256→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   257→    if effective_strategy:
   258→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   259→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   260→
   261→
   262→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   263→# Stage handlers
   264→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   265→
   266→def _cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   267→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
   268→
   269→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
   270→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
   271→    """
   272→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   273→
   274→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   275→    state = runtime.state
   276→    plan = load_plan()
   277→
   278→    # Auto-start: inject synthesis::pending if not present
   279→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   280→        order = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   281→        order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   282→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   283→        meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
   284→        save_plan(plan)
   285→        print(colorize("  Synthesis auto-started (injected synthesis::pending).", "cyan"))
   286→
   287→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   288→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   289→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
   290→
   291→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   292→    is_reuse = False
   293→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   294→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   295→        is_reuse = True
   296→    elif not report:
   297→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage observe.", "red"))
   298→        print(colorize("  Write an analysis of the findings: themes, root causes, contradictions.", "dim"))
   299→        print(colorize("  Identify findings that contradict each other (opposite recommendations).", "dim"))
   300→        print(colorize("  Do NOT just list finding IDs — describe what you actually observe.", "dim"))
   301→        return
   302→
   303→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   304→    finding_count = len(si.open_findings)
   305→
   306→    # Edge case: 0 findings
   307→    if finding_count == 0:
   308→        stages["observe"] = {
   309→            "stage": "observe",
   310→            "report": report,
   311→            "cited_ids": [],
   312→            "timestamp": utc_now(),
   313→            "finding_count": 0,
   314→        }
   315→        if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   316→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   317→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   318→        cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
   319→        if not is_reuse:
   320→            stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
   321→            stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
   322→        save_plan(plan)
   323→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no findings to analyse).", "green"))
   324→        if is_reuse:
   325→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   326→            if cleared:
   327→                _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   328→        return
   329→
   330→    # Validation: report length (no citation counting)
   331→    min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100
   332→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   333→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   334→        print(colorize("  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how findings relate.", "dim"))
   335→        return
   336→
   337→    # Save stage (still extract citations for analytics, but don't gate on them)
   338→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   339→    cited = extract_finding_citations(report, valid_ids)
   340→
   341→    stages["observe"] = {
   342→        "stage": "observe",
   343→        "report": report,
   344→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited),
   345→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   346→        "finding_count": finding_count,
   347→    }
   348→
   349→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   350→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   351→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   352→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   353→    cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
   354→
   355→    save_plan(plan)
   356→
   357→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_observe", actor="user",
   358→                     detail={"finding_count": finding_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited),
   359→                             "reuse": is_reuse})
   360→    save_plan(plan)
   361→
   362→    print(colorize(
   363→        f"  Observe stage recorded: {finding_count} findings analysed.",
   364→        "green",
   365→    ))
   366→    if is_reuse:
   367→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   368→        if cleared:
   369→            _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   370→    else:
   371→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   372→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm observe", "dim"))
   373→
   374→
   375→def _cmd_stage_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

   378→    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether
```

```
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}

    77→    updated: Required[str]
    78→    queue_order: list[str]
```
