```
    91→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    92→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    93→    now = utc_now()
    94→    return {
    95→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    96→        "created": now,
    97→        "updated": now,
    98→        "queue_order": [],
    99→        "deferred": [],
   100→        "skipped": {},
   101→        "active_cluster": None,
   102→        "overrides": {},
   103→        "clusters": {},
   104→        "superseded": {},
   105→        "promoted_ids": [],
   106→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   107→        "execution_log": [],
   108→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   109→    }
   110→
   111→
   112→def _ensure_container_types(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   113→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   114→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   115→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   116→        plan["deferred"] = []
   117→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   118→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   119→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   120→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   121→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   122→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   123→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   124→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   125→    if not isinstance(plan.get("promoted_ids"), list):
   126→        plan["promoted_ids"] = []
   127→    if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict):
   128→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   129→    if not isinstance(plan.get("execution_log"), list):
   130→        plan["execution_log"] = []
   131→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   132→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   133→
   134→
   135→def _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   136→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   137→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   138→    if not deferred:
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    now = utc_now()
   142→    for fid in list(deferred):
   143→        if fid in skipped:
   144→            continue
   145→        skipped[fid] = {
   146→            "finding_id": fid,
   147→            "kind": "temporary",
   148→            "reason": None,
   149→            "note": None,
   150→            "attestation": None,
   151→            "created_at": now,
   152→            "review_after": None,
   153→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   154→        }
   155→    deferred.clear()
   156→
   157→
   158→def _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   159→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   160→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   161→            continue
   162→        if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   163→            cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   164→        cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   165→        cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   166→        cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   167→        cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   171→    """Migrate v3 top-level ``epics`` dict into ``clusters`` (v4 unification)."""
   172→    epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   173→    if not isinstance(epics, dict) or not epics:
   174→        return
   175→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   176→    now = utc_now()
   177→    for name, epic in epics.items():
   178→        if not isinstance(epic, dict):
   179→            continue
   180→        if name in clusters:
   181→            continue  # don't overwrite existing cluster
   182→        clusters[name] = {
   183→            "name": name,
   184→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   185→            "finding_ids": epic.get("finding_ids", []),
   186→            "auto": True,
   187→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
   188→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
   189→            "user_modified": False,
   190→            "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
   191→            "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
   192→            # Epic-specific fields
   193→            "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   194→            "direction": epic.get("direction", "simplify"),
   195→            "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   196→            "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
   197→            "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
   198→            "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   199→            "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   200→            "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   201→            "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
   202→            "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   203→            "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   204→        }
   205→
   206→
   207→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   208→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   209→
   210→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   211→    and v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters).
   212→    """
   213→    defaults = empty_plan()
   214→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   215→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   216→
   217→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   218→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   219→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   220→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   221→
   222→
   223→def synthesis_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   224→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   225→    return {
   226→        name: cluster
   227→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   228→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   229→    }
   230→
   231→
   232→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   233→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   234→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   235→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   236→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   237→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   238→
   239→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   240→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   241→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   242→    if overlap:
   243→        raise ValueError(
   244→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   245→        )
   246→
   247→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   248→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   249→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   250→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   251→            raise ValueError(
   252→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   253→            )
   254→
   255→
   256→__all__ = [
   257→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   258→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   259→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   260→    "Cluster",
   261→    "ItemOverride",
   262→    "PlanModel",
   263→    "SkipEntry",
   264→    "SupersededEntry",
   265→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   266→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   267→    "empty_plan",
   268→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   269→    "synthesis_clusters",
   270→    "validate_plan",
   271→]
   272→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass
    16→class ReconcileResult:
    17→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    18→
    19→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    20→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    changes: int = 0
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_candidates(
    26→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    27→) -> list[str]:
    28→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    29→    candidates: list[str] = []
    30→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    31→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    32→            continue
    33→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    34→            candidates.append(fid)
    35→    return candidates
    36→
    37→
    38→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    40→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    41→    if finding is None:
    42→        return False
    43→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _supersede_id(
    47→    plan: PlanModel,
    48→    state: StateModel,
    49→    finding_id: str,
    50→    now: str,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    53→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    54→    detector = ""
    55→    file = ""
    56→    summary = ""
    57→    if finding:
    58→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    60→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    61→
    62→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    63→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    64→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    65→
    66→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    67→        "original_id": finding_id,
    68→        "original_detector": detector,
    69→        "original_file": file,
    70→        "original_summary": summary,
    71→        "status": "superseded",
    72→        "superseded_at": now,
    73→        "remapped_to": None,
    74→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Preserve any existing override note
    78→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    79→    if override and override.get("note"):
    80→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    81→
    82→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    83→
    84→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster finding_ids
    85→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    86→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    87→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    if finding_id in promoted:
    92→        promoted.remove(finding_id)
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    95→        if finding_id in ids:
    96→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    97→
    98→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
    99→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
   100→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   101→        override["cluster"] = None
   102→        override["updated_at"] = now
   103→
   104→    return True
   105→
   106→
   107→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   108→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   109→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   110→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   111→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   112→
   113→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   114→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   115→        try:
   116→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   117→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   118→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   119→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   120→                to_prune.append(fid)
   121→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   122→            to_prune.append(fid)
   123→
   124→    for fid in to_prune:
   125→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   126→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   127→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   128→
   129→    return to_prune
   130→
   131→
   132→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   133→    plan: PlanModel,
   134→    state: StateModel,
   135→) -> ReconcileResult:
   136→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   137→
   138→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   139→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   140→    """
   141→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   142→    result = ReconcileResult()
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   145→
   146→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   147→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   148→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   150→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   151→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   152→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   153→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   154→
   155→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   156→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   157→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   158→    referenced_ids = {
   159→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   160→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   161→        and fid != SYNTHESIS_ID
   162→    }
   163→
   164→    # Check each referenced ID
   165→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   166→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   167→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   168→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   169→                result.changes += 1
   170→
   171→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   172→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   173→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   174→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   175→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   176→            continue
   177→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   178→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   179→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   180→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   181→            result.changes += 1
   182→        if not alive_ids:
   183→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   184→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   185→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   186→        result.changes += 1
   187→
   188→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   189→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   190→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   191→
   192→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   193→    if resurfaced:
   194→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   195→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   196→
   197→    # Prune old superseded entries
   198→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   199→    result.pruned = pruned
   200→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   201→
   202→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   203→    if result.changes > 0:
   204→        from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
   205→
   206→        append_log_entry(
   207→            plan,
   208→            "reconcile",
   209→            finding_ids=result.superseded,
   210→            actor="system",
   211→            detail={
   212→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   213→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   214→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   215→            },
   216→        )
   217→
   218→    return result
   219→
   220→
   221→@dataclass
   222→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   223→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   224→
   225→    new_ids: set[str]
   226→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   227→    synthesis_injected: bool
   228→
   229→
   230→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   231→    plan: PlanModel,
   232→    state: StateModel,
   233→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   234→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   235→
   236→    Appends new finding IDs to queue_order and injects synthesis::pending
   237→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new findings to sync.
   238→    """
   239→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   240→        compute_new_finding_ids,
   241→        sync_synthesis_needed,
   242→    )
   243→
   244→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   245→    new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   246→    if not new_ids:
   247→        return None
   248→
   249→    # Add new finding IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   250→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   251→    existing = set(order)
   252→    added: list[str] = []
   253→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   254→        if fid not in existing:
   255→            order.append(fid)
   256→            added.append(fid)
   257→
   258→    # Inject synthesis::pending if needed
   259→    synthesis_result = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, state)
   260→    synthesis_injected = bool(
   261→        synthesis_result and getattr(synthesis_result, "injected", False)
   262→    )
   263→
   264→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   265→        new_ids=new_ids,
   266→        added_to_queue=added,
   267→        synthesis_injected=synthesis_injected,
   268→    )
   269→
   270→
   271→__all__ = [
   272→    "ReconcileResult",
   273→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   274→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   275→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   276→]
   277→
```

```
1→"""Handler for ``plan synthesize`` subcommand.
     2→
     3→Implements a staged workflow: OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT
     4→with a fast-track CONFIRM-EXISTING skip path.
     5→
     6→Stage gates validate **plan data enrichment**, not text reports:
     7→- OBSERVE: lightweight — write an analysis of themes/root causes (100 char min)
     8→- REFLECT: compare current findings against completed work (recurring patterns)
     9→- ORGANIZE: structural — each manual cluster must have description + action_steps
    10→- COMMIT: strategy must be substantive (200 char min) or "same"
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations

    21→    SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
    22→    SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
    23→    SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD,
    24→    SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE,
    25→    SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT,

    32→)

    34→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    35→from desloppify.engine._plan.epic_synthesis import (
    36→    build_synthesis_prompt,
    37→    collect_synthesis_input,
    38→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    39→    extract_finding_citations,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
    42→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    43→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    44→from desloppify.engine.plan import SYNTHESIS_ID, load_plan, save_plan
    45→
    46→
    47→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]
    48→
    49→
    50→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    51→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    52→    try:
    53→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    54→    except ValueError:
    55→        return []
    56→    cleared: list[str] = []
    57→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    58→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    59→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    60→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    61→            cleared.append(later)
    62→    return cleared
    63→
    64→
    65→def _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    66→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    67→    if not cleared:
    68→        return
    69→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    70→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    71→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    72→        None,
    73→    )
    74→    if next_unconfirmed:
    75→        print(colorize(
    76→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan synthesize --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    77→            "dim",
    78→        ))
    79→
    80→
    81→def _observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    82→    """Count findings per dimension from a SynthesisInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    83→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    84→    for _fid, f in si.open_findings.items():
    85→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    86→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    87→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    88→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    89→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    90→
    91→
    92→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    93→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review findings in synthesis."""
    94→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    95→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    96→    for c in clusters.values():
    97→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→    review_ids = [
    99→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   100→        if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
```

```
def test_hash_ignores_non_review(self):
        state = {
            "findings": {
                "unused::a": {"status": "open", "detector": "unused"},
                "review::b": {"status": "open", "detector": "review"},
            }
        }
        h = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
        assert h != ""
        # Should only include review::b
        state2 = _state_with_review_findings("review::b")
        assert review_finding_snapshot_hash(state2) == h

    def test_hash_ignores_closed(self):
        state = {
            "findings": {
                "review::a": {"status": "fixed", "detector": "review"},
            }
        }
        assert review_finding_snapshot_hash(state) == ""
```

```
1→"""Shared display utilities for command output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def short_finding_id(fid: str) -> str:
     7→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display.
     8→
     9→    Finding IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    10→    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    11→    """
    12→    if "::" in fid:
    13→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    14→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    15→            return suffix[:8]
    16→    return fid
    17→
    18→
    19→__all__ = ["short_finding_id"]
    20→
```

```
   445→
   446→    # Detect recurring patterns
   447→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(si.open_findings, si.resolved_findings)
   448→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
   449→
   450→    # If recurring patterns exist, report must mention at least one dimension
   451→    if recurring_dims:
   452→        report_lower = report.lower()
   453→        mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
   454→        if not mentioned:
   455→            print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
   456→            for dim in recurring_dims:
   457→                info = recurring[dim]
   458→                print(colorize(
   459→                    f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   460→                    f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   461→                    "yellow",
   462→                ))
   463→            print(colorize(
   464→                "  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.",
   465→                "dim",
   466→            ))
   467→            return
   468→
   469→    # Save stage
   470→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   471→    stages["reflect"] = {
   472→        "stage": "reflect",
   473→        "report": report,
   474→        "cited_ids": [],
   475→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   476→        "finding_count": finding_count,
   477→        "recurring_dimensions": recurring_dims,
   478→    }
   479→
   480→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   481→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   482→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   483→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   484→    cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   485→
   486→    save_plan(plan)
   487→
   488→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_reflect", actor="user",
   489→                     detail={"finding_count": finding_count, "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   490→                             "reuse": is_reuse})
   491→    save_plan(plan)
   492→
   493→    print(colorize(
   494→        f"  Reflect stage recorded: {finding_count} findings, "
   495→        f"{len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).",
   496→        "green",
   497→    ))
   498→    if is_reuse:
   499→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
```

```
1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing synthesis-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→import re
    13→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    17→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    18→    VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS,
    19→    Cluster,
    20→    PlanModel,
    21→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    22→    synthesis_clusters,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    25→    SYNTHESIS_ID,
    26→    review_finding_snapshot_hash,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Finding-ID citation extraction
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→FINDING_ID_RE = re.compile(r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}")
    38→
    39→
    40→def extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    41→    """Extract finding IDs cited in free text.
    42→
    43→    Matches full finding IDs (e.g. ``review::abcdef12``) or bare 8+ char
    44→    hex suffixes that correspond to a known finding.
    45→    """
    46→    cited: set[str] = set()
    47→    # Match full finding IDs
    48→    for match in FINDING_ID_RE.finditer(text):
    49→        candidate = match.group()
    50→        if candidate in valid_ids:
    51→            cited.add(candidate)
    52→    # Match 8+ char hex suffixes
    53→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", text):
    54→        for valid_id in valid_ids:
    55→            if valid_id.endswith("::" + token):
    56→                cited.add(valid_id)
    57→                break
    58→    return cited
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# Completed-work helpers
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    66→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    67→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    68→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    69→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    70→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    71→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    72→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    73→                if ts:
    74→                    return str(ts)
    75→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    76→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    77→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→
    81→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    82→    open_findings: dict[str, dict],
    83→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict],
    84→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    85→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open findings.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    88→    A dimension with both resolved and open findings signals a potential
    89→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    90→    """
    91→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    92→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    93→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    94→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    98→    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
    99→        dim = _dimension(f)
   100→        if dim:
   101→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   102→
   103→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   104→    for fid, f in resolved_findings.items():
   105→        dim = _dimension(f)
   106→        if dim:
   107→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   108→
   109→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
   110→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
   111→        recurring[dim] = {
   112→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
   113→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
   114→        }
   115→    return recurring
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Data types
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→@dataclass
   123→class SynthesisInput:
   124→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
   125→
   126→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
   127→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
   128→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
   129→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
   130→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
   131→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
   132→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
   133→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
   134→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict]   # full finding objects for resolved IDs
   135→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last synthesis
   136→
   137→
   138→@dataclass
   139→class DismissedFinding:
   140→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
   141→
   142→    finding_id: str
   143→    reason: str
   144→
   145→
   146→@dataclass
   147→class ContradictionNote:
   148→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
   149→
   150→    kept: str
   151→    dismissed: str
   152→    reason: str
   153→
   154→
   155→@dataclass
   156→class SynthesisResult:
   157→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
   158→
   159→    strategy_summary: str
   160→    epics: list[dict]
   161→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
   162→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
   163→    priority_rationale: str = ""
   164→
   165→
   166→@dataclass
   167→class SynthesisMutationResult:
   168→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
   169→
   170→    epics_created: int = 0
   171→    epics_updated: int = 0
   172→    epics_completed: int = 0
   173→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
   174→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
   175→    strategy_summary: str = ""
   176→    synthesis_version: int = 0
   177→    dry_run: bool = False
   178→
   179→
   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→# Input collection
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→
   184→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
   185→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
   186→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   187→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   188→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   189→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   190→
   191→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   192→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   193→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   194→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   195→            continue
   196→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   197→            open_review[fid] = f
   198→        else:
   199→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   200→
   201→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   202→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   203→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   204→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   205→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   206→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   207→
   208→    # Resolved finding objects (for REFLECT stage)
   209→    resolved_finding_objs = {
   210→        fid: findings[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in findings
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    # Completed clusters since last synthesis completion
   214→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   215→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   216→    if last_completed:
   217→        recent_completed = [
   218→            c for c in all_completed
   219→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   220→        ]
   221→    else:
   222→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
   223→
   224→    return SynthesisInput(
   225→        open_findings=open_review,
   226→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   227→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   228→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   229→        new_since_last=new_since,
   230→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   231→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   232→        synthesis_version=version,
   233→        resolved_findings=resolved_finding_objs,
   234→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→
   238→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   239→# Prompt construction
   240→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   241→
   242→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   243→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   244→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   245→
   246→Your plan should:
   247→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   248→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   249→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   250→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   251→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   252→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   253→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   254→
   255→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   256→
   257→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   258→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   259→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   260→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   261→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   262→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   263→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   264→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   265→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   266→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   267→
   268→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   269→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   270→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   271→
   272→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   273→{
   274→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   275→  "epics": [
   276→    {
   277→      "name": "slug-name",
   278→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   279→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   280→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   281→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   282→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   283→      "agent_safe": true,
   284→      "dependency_order": 1,
   285→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   286→      "status": "pending"
   287→    }
   288→  ],
   289→  "dismissed_findings": [
   290→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}

   292→  "contradiction_notes": [
   293→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   294→  ],
   295→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   296→}
   297→"""
   298→
   299→
   300→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
```

```
1→"""Organize stage handler for plan synthesis."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis import shared as synth_shared_mod
     9→from desloppify.core.output import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→
    12→
    13→def cmd_stage_organize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    14→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
    15→    plan = load_plan()
    16→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    17→
    18→    if not synth_shared_mod._require_synthesis_pending(plan, action="organize"):
    19→        return
    20→
    21→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    22→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    23→    if "reflect" not in stages:
    24→        if "observe" not in stages:
    25→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    26→            print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    27→        else:
    28→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
    29→            print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
    30→        return
    31→
    32→    manual_clusters = synth_shared_mod._manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
    33→    if not manual_clusters:
    34→        any_clusters = [
    35→            name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if cluster.get("finding_ids")
    36→        ]
    37→        if any_clusters:
    38→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
    39→            print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group findings by root cause:", "dim"))
    40→        else:
    41→            print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with findings exist.", "red"))
    42→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    43→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <finding-patterns>", "dim"))
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    gaps = synth_shared_mod._unenriched_clusters(plan)
    47→    if gaps:
    48→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
    49→        for name, missing in gaps:
    50→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    51→        print()
    52→        print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" '
    56→                '--steps "step 1" "step 2"',
    57→                "dim",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    63→    validated_report = synth_shared_mod._validate_stage_report(
    64→        report,
    65→        stage="organize",
    66→        min_chars=100,
    67→        missing_guidance=[
    68→            "Summarize your prioritized organization:",
    69→            "- Did you defer contradictory findings before clustering?",
    70→            "- What clusters did you create and why?",
    71→            "- Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?",
    72→            "- What depends on what? What unblocks the most?",
    73→        ],
    74→        short_guidance=[
    75→            "Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.",
    76→        ],
    77→    )
    78→    if validated_report is None:
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    synth_shared_mod.record_synthesis_stage(
    82→        plan,
    83→        state,
    84→        stage="organize",
    85→        report=validated_report,
    86→        cited_ids=[],
    87→        finding_count=len(manual_clusters),
    88→    )
    89→
    90→    print(
    91→        colorize(
    92→            f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).",
    93→            "green",
    94→        )
    95→    )
    96→    for name in manual_clusters:
    97→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
    98→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    99→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   100→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   101→        print(
   102→            colorize(
   103→                f"    {name}: {len(cluster.get('finding_ids', []))} findings, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}",
   104→                "dim",
   105→            )
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    print()
   109→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Prioritized organization (share with user) ───────────┐", "cyan"))
   110→    for line in validated_report.strip().splitlines():
   111→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   112→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   105→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   106→
   107→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   108→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   109→    """
   110→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   111→    if not promoted:
   112→        return 0
   113→    last_idx = -1
   114→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   115→        if fid in promoted:
   116→            last_idx = i
   117→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   118→
   119→
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   122→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→
   124→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   128→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   129→
   130→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   131→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   132→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   133→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   134→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   135→    """
   136→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   137→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   138→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   139→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   140→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   141→
   142→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   143→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   144→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   145→        fid for fid in order
   146→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   147→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   148→        and fid not in stale_ids
   149→    ]
   150→    for fid in to_remove:
   151→        order.remove(fid)
   152→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   153→
   154→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   155→    existing = set(order)
   156→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   157→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   158→        if uid not in existing:
   159→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   160→            result.injected.append(uid)
   161→
   162→    return result
   163→
   164→
   165→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   167→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→
   169→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   170→    plan: PlanModel,
   171→    state: StateModel,
   172→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   173→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   174→
   175→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   176→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   177→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   178→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   179→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   180→    """
   181→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   182→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   183→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   184→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in stale_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   200→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   201→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   202→        existing = set(order)
   203→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   204→            if sid not in existing:
   205→                order.append(sid)
   206→                result.injected.append(sid)
   207→
   208→    return result
   209→
   210→
   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→
   215→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   216→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   217→
   218→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   219→    """
   220→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   221→    review_ids = sorted(
   222→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   223→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   224→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   225→    )
   226→    if not review_ids:
   227→        return ""
   228→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   229→
   230→
   231→@dataclass
   232→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   233→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   234→
   235→    injected: bool = False
   236→    pruned: bool = False
   237→
   238→    @property
   239→    def changes(self) -> int:
   240→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   241→
   242→
   243→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   244→    plan: PlanModel,
   245→    state: StateModel,
   246→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   247→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   248→
   249→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   250→    """
   251→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   252→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   253→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   254→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   255→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   256→
   257→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   258→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   259→
   260→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject after promoted items
   261→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   262→        order.insert(_after_promoted(order, plan), SYNTHESIS_ID)
   263→        result.injected = True
   264→
   265→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   266→
   267→    return result
   268→
   269→
   270→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   271→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   272→
   273→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   274→    """
   275→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   276→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   277→    current = {
   278→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   279→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   280→    }
   281→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   282→
   283→
   284→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   285→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   286→
   287→    Returns True when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue OR the
   288→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   289→    """
   290→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   291→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   292→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   293→        return True
   294→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   295→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   296→    if not last_hash:
   297→        return False
   298→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   299→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   300→
   301→
   302→__all__ = [
   303→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   304→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   305→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   306→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   307→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   308→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   309→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   310→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   311→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   312→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   313→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   314→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   315→]
   316→
```

```
    84→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    85→    """Return a banner string when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue."""
    86→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    87→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    88→        return ""
    89→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    90→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    91→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
    92→    if completed:
    93→        return (
    94→            f"Synthesis in progress ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete). "
    95→            "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    96→        )
    97→    return (
    98→        "Synthesis needed — review findings require analysis before fixing. "
    99→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
   100→    )
   101→

   104→    # schema

   106→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   107→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   108→    "Cluster",
   109→    "ItemOverride",
```

```
    47→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    48→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    49→
    50→    items: list[dict]

    61→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    62→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    63→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    73→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    74→        return items
    75→
    76→    subjective_items = deque(
    77→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    78→    )
    79→    objective_items = deque(
    80→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    81→    )
    82→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    83→        return items
    84→
    85→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    86→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    87→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    88→            if not objective_items:
    89→                break
    90→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    91→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    92→            if not subjective_items:
    93→                break
    94→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    95→
    96→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    97→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    98→    return interleaved
    99→
   100→
   101→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   102→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
   103→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   104→    if not scan_history:
   105→        return set()
   106→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
   107→    if not threshold:
   108→        return set()
   109→    return {
   110→        fid
   111→        for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   112→        if f.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
   113→    }
   114→
   115→
   116→def _apply_plan_order(
   117→    items: list[dict],
   118→    plan: dict,
   119→    *,
   120→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   121→    cluster: str | None = None,
   122→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   123→) -> list[dict]:
   124→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   125→
   126→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   127→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   128→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   129→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   130→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   131→    """
   132→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   133→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   134→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   135→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   136→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   137→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   138→
   139→    # Build lookup
   140→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   141→    for item in items:
   142→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   143→
   144→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   145→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   146→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   147→        if override.get("description"):
   148→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   149→        if override.get("note"):
   150→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   151→        if override.get("cluster"):
   152→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   153→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   154→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   155→                "name": cluster_name,
   156→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   157→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   158→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   159→            }
   160→
   161→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   162→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   163→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   164→    for fid in queue_order:
   165→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   166→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   167→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   168→
   169→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   170→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   171→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   172→    _new = new_ids or set()
   173→    for item in items:
   174→        item_id = item["id"]
   175→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   176→            continue
   177→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   178→            skipped_items.append(item)
   179→        elif item_id in _new:
   180→            remaining_new.append(item)
   181→        else:
   182→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   183→
   184→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   185→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   186→    if include_skipped:
   187→        result = result + skipped_items
   188→
   189→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   190→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   191→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   192→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   193→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   194→            if skip_entry:
   195→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   196→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   197→                if skip_reason:
   198→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   199→
   200→    # Filter to cluster if requested
```

```
318→def build_work_queue(
   319→    state: StateModel,
   320→    *,
   321→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   322→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   323→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   324→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   325→
   326→    status = resolved_options.status
   327→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   328→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   329→    try:
   330→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   331→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   332→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   333→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   334→
   335→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   336→        state,
   337→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   338→        status_filter=status,
   339→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   340→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   344→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   345→    suppressed_count = 0
   346→    if (
   347→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   348→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   349→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   350→    ):
   351→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   352→            state,
   353→            state.get("findings", {}),
   354→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   355→        )
   356→        for item in subjective_items:
   357→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   358→                continue
   359→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   360→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   361→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   362→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   363→                suppressed_count += 1
   364→                continue
   365→            all_items.append(item)
   366→
   367→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   368→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   369→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   370→        if synth_item is not None:
   371→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   372→
   373→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   374→
   375→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   376→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   377→    if resolved_options.plan:
   378→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   379→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   380→            all_items,
   381→            resolved_options.plan,
   382→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   383→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   384→            new_ids=new_ids,
   385→        )
   386→
   387→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   388→    should_collapse = (
   389→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   390→        and resolved_options.plan
   391→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   392→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   393→    )
   394→    if should_collapse:
   395→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   396→
   397→    should_interleave = (
   398→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   399→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   400→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   401→        and resolved_options.tier is None
   402→        and not resolved_options.scope
   403→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   404→    )
   405→    if should_interleave:
   406→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   407→
   408→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
```

## Exploration Summary

I've identified both issues in your codebase. Here are the findings:

### Issue 1: Cluster Overlap Warning Inefficiency (cluster_handlers.py)

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`, lines 73-87 in `_cmd_cluster_add()`

**The Problem:**

```python
# Line 74: member_set contains ALL current members, including pre-existing ones
member_set = set(plan["clusters"][cluster_name].get("finding_ids", []))
for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):
        continue
    other_ids = set(other_cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    if not other_ids:
        continue
    overlap = member_set & other_ids
    if len(overlap) > len(other_ids) * 0.5:
        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(overlap)} finding(s) also in cluster '{other_name}' ..."))
```

**What's Happening:**
- Line 62 resolves `finding_ids` (the IDs being added - let's say 1 new finding)
- Line 68 calls `add_to_cluster()` which adds those IDs to the cluster
- Line 74 fetches ALL members in the cluster after the addition
- The overlap check then compares this **complete member set** (old + new) against every other cluster
- This causes the warning to fire for **pre-existing overlaps** that weren't caused by this operation

**Example:** Adding 1 finding to a cluster with 50 existing members that already had 25 overlapping findings with another cluster would trigger a warning saying "25 findings also in cluster X" — but you only added 1, not 25.

**How to fix it:** Check overlap only for `finding_ids` (the newly-added IDs), not the entire `member_set`:
```python
overlap = set(finding_ids) & other_ids  # Only check NEW findings
```

---

### Issue 2: Step Display Double-Numbering

**Location 1:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`, lines 263-265 in `_cmd_cluster_update()`

```python
print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    print(colorize(f"    {i}. {step}", "dim"))
```

**The Problem:** When a user provides steps like `"1. Audit dependencies"`, the enumerate adds another "1." prefix, producing `"1. 1. Audit dependencies"`.

**Why it happens:** 
- `enumerate(steps, 1)` generates indices 1, 2, 3, ...
- The format string `f"    {i}. {step}"` prepends the index
- If the user's step text already starts with "1. ", "2. ", etc., you get "1. 1. ..."

**Related display locations** (where steps are shown but NOT enumerated again):
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`, line 1052-1053: Uses bullet points (`- {step}`) — correct
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis/shared.py`, line 268-269: Uses bullet points (`- {step}`) — correct
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`, line 1289: Enumerates queue items but NOT cluster steps — correct

**How to fix it:** Either:
1. Strip user-provided numbering before storage, OR
2. Display steps without enumeration (use bullet points like other display code does), OR
3. Use a regex to detect pre-numbered steps and skip adding indices

The cleanest approach would be to store steps without numbering and always display them with consistent formatting (bullets or auto-numbered by the display layer).

```
1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    25→
    26→
    27→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    28→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    29→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    30→    if not plan_path.exists():
    31→        return empty_plan()
    32→
    33→    try:
    34→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    35→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    36→        # Try backup before giving up
    37→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    38→        if backup.exists():
    39→            try:
    40→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
    41→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
    42→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
    43→                # Fall through to validation below
    44→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
    45→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
    46→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    47→                return empty_plan()
    48→        else:
    49→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    50→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    51→            return empty_plan()
    52→
    53→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    54→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
    55→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    56→        return empty_plan()
    57→
    58→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    59→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    60→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
    61→        print(
    62→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    63→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    64→            file=sys.stderr,
    65→        )
    66→
    67→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    68→    try:
    69→        validate_plan(data)
    70→    except ValueError as ex:
    71→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    72→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    73→        return empty_plan()
    74→
    75→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    76→
    77→
    78→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    79→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    80→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    81→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    82→    validate_plan(plan)
    83→
    84→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    85→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    86→
    87→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    88→
    89→    if plan_path.exists():
    90→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    91→        try:
    92→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    93→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    94→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    95→
    96→    try:
    97→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    98→    except OSError as ex:
    99→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   100→        raise
   101→
   102→
   103→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   104→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   105→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   106→
   107→
   108→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   109→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   110→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
   111→    if not plan_path.exists():
   112→        return False
   113→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   114→    return bool(
   115→        plan.get("queue_order")
   116→        or plan.get("overrides")
   117→        or plan.get("clusters")
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "PLAN_FILE",
   123→    "has_living_plan",
   124→    "load_plan",
   125→    "plan_path_for_state",
   126→    "save_plan",
   127→]
   128→
```

```
217→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   218→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove synthesis::pending, save."""
   219→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   220→
   221→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   222→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   223→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   224→

   226→    state = runtime.state

   228→
   229→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   230→    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   231→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   232→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   233→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   234→    )
   235→    meta["synthesized_ids"] = open_review_ids
   236→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   237→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   238→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
   239→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   240→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   241→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   242→    if stages:
   243→        meta["last_synthesis"] = {
   244→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   245→            "stages": {k: dict(v) for k, v in stages.items()},
   246→            "strategy": strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", ""),
   247→        }
   248→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   249→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   250→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   251→
   252→    save_plan(plan)
   253→
   254→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   255→    print(colorize(f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   256→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   257→    if effective_strategy:
   258→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   259→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))

   261→
   262→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
```
